definition{
  name: administrationResultMatches,
  parameters: [
    result : (Result Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationSnapshot Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError),
    expected : Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationSnapshot
  ],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match result
    | Result.error error => false
    | Result.ok actual => ((prim equal (project actual organization) (project expected organization) : Bool) && ((prim equal (project actual revision) (project expected revision) : Bool) && ((Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::administrationSamePlan (project actual plan) (project expected plan)) && true)))),
  axioms: []
}

definition{
  name: administrationResultError,
  parameters: [
    result : (Result Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationSnapshot Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError),
    expected : Nat
  ],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match result
    | Result.ok actual => false
    | Result.error error => (match error
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.BadState => (prim equal expected 0 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.ResourceLimit => (prim equal expected 1 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.WrongOrganization => (prim equal expected 2 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.StaleRevision => (prim equal expected 3 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.RevisionExhausted => (prim equal expected 4 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.BadRequest => (prim equal expected 5 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.RootChanged => (prim equal expected 6 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.StageRollback => (prim equal expected 7 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.ScopeRemoval => (prim equal expected 8 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.BadProposal => (prim equal expected 9 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.NoChange => (prim equal expected 10 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.BadApproval => (prim equal expected 11 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.DuplicateApproval => (prim equal expected 12 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.OutOfScopeApproval => (prim equal expected 13 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.InsufficientApproval => (prim equal expected 14 : Bool)
      | Foundation.Organization.V1.Administration::AdministrationError.CoverageLoss => (prim equal expected 15 : Bool))),
  axioms: []
}
